problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # JADE external archive and success-history memory\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim), dtype=float)\n        archive_size = max(pop_size, 10)\n        memory_len = 5\n        memory_F = np.full(memory_len, float(self.F))\n        memory_CR = np.full(memory_len, float(self.CR))\n        memory_pos = 0\n = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # JADE success-history memory parameters\n            # Controller-provided F/CR act as initial memory defaults and exploration bias\n            F_ctrl = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR_ctrl = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            p_best = float(params.get('p_best', p_best))\n\n            # Optional: use controller values to bias this generation's memory sampling\n            gen_memory_F = np.concatenate([memory_F, np.array([F_ctrl])])\n            gen_memory_CR = np.concatenate([memory_CR, np.array([CR_ctrl])])\n\n            SF = []\n            SCR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sorted indices for p-best selection (pbest Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution around a random memory entry\n                attempts = 0\n                F_i = F_ctrl\n                while attempts < 20:\n                    m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_F.shape[0]))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + gen_memory_F[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i > 0.0:\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                F_i_list[i] = F_i\n\n                # Sample CR_i from normal distribution around a random memory entry\n                m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_CR.shape[0]))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.randn() * 0.1 + gen_memory_CR[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                CR_i_list[i] = CR_i\n\n                # Choose p-best from top p*pop_size individuals\n                p_num = max(1, int(round(p_best * pop_size)))\n                p_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_num)])\n                x_best_p = pop[p_idx]\n\n                # Select two distinct random indices from pop and archive\n                # Use union of pop + archive for greater diversity\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if candidates:\n                    r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n\n                # Choose r2 from union(pop, archive), cannot equal i or r1\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\n                        x_r2 = archive[r2]\n                    else:\n                        r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n mean for F\n                SF_arr = np.array(SF, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr * SF_arr))\n                denom_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr))\n t) + self.w_end * t\\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\\n        F = 0.9 - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip to bounds\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors with JADE selection and archive updates\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with archive\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old position before replacing\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_size:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n                    else:\n                        # Replace a random archive member\n                        replace_pos = int(rng.randint(0, archive_size))\n                        archive[replace_pos] = pop[i]\n\n                    # Track successful parameter values\n                    SF.append(F_i_list[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_i_list[i])\n                    S_delta.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE success-history memory\n            if len(SF) > 0:\n                deltas = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                weights = deltas / (np.sum(deltas) + 1e-12)\n\n                # Lehmer mean for F\n                SF_arr = np.array(SF, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr * SF_arr))\n                denom_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr))\n                if denom_F > 1e-12:\n                    memory_F[memory_pos] = min(1.0, max(0.0, sum_F / denom_F))\n\n                # Weighted mean for CR (standard mean)\n                SCR_arr = np.array(SCR, dtype=float)\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(np.sum(weights * SCR_arr), 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_len\n\n            # Update stagnation counter based on this generation's improvement\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n                stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                stagnation_count += 1\n\n            # Partial diversity restart when the run has stalled for a while.\n            # Replace the worst half of the population with fresh random points.\n            if stagnation_count >= 15 and evals < self.max_evals - pop_size:\n                restart_count = max(1, pop_size // 2)\n                # Keep the current global best individual in the population\n                cur_best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n                if gbest_f < fitness[cur_best_idx]:\n                    pop[cur_best_idx] = gbest.copy()\n                    fitness[cur_best_idx] = gbest_f\n                # Replace the worst individuals (excluding the preserved best) with random points\n                order = np.argsort(fitness)\n                worst_idx = order[-restart_count:]\n                for idx in worst_idx:\n                    if idx == cur_best_idx:\n                        continue\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                        break\n                    new_x = rng.uniform(lb, ub)\n                    pop[idx] = new_x\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, new_x)\n                    evals += 1\n                stagnation_count = 0\n                # Re-sort for next iteration's p-best selection\n                sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\"\n    }\n    </json>",

The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # JADE external archive and success-history memory\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim), dtype=float)\n        archive_size = max(pop_size, 10)\n        memory_len = 5\n        memory_F = np.full(memory_len, float(self.F))\n        memory_CR = np.full(memory_len, float(self.CR))\n        memory_pos = 0\n        initial_p_best = float(self.params.get('p_best', 0.2))\n        min_p_best = max(0.05, initial_p_best * 0.25)\n        p_best = initial_p_best\n\n        # Stagnation tracking for diversity-triggered restarts\n        stagnation_count = 0\n\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # JADE success-history memory parameters\n            # Controller-provided F/CR act as initial memory defaults and exploration bias\n            F_ctrl = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR_ctrl = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n\n            # Progress-based schedule: reduce p_best over time for finer exploitation\n            progress = float(evals) / float(max(1, self.max_evals))\n            if 'p_best' in params:\n                p_best = float(params.get('p_best'))\n            else:\n                p_best = initial_p_best - (initial_p_best - min_p_best) * progress\n\n            # If no recent improvement, add an exploration bias to F/CR\n            if stagnation_count > 0:\n                exploration_bias = min(0.4, 0.1 * stagnation_count)\n                F_ctrl = min(1.0, F_ctrl + exploration_bias)\n                CR_ctrl = min(1.0, CR_ctrl + exploration_bias)\n\n            # Use the archive more as the run progresses (diversity reservoir)\n            archive_prob = 0.5 + 0.3 * progress\n\n            # Optional: use controller values to bias this generation's memory sampling\n            gen_memory_F = np.concatenate([memory_F, np.array([F_ctrl])])\n            gen_memory_CR = np.concatenate([memory_CR, np.array([CR_ctrl])])\n\n            SF = []\n            SCR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sorted indices for p-best selection (pbest mutation)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE (current-to-pbest/1)\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Cauchy/randn mutation factors per individual\n            F_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n            CR_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution around a random memory entry\n                attempts = 0\n                F_i = F_ctrl\n                while attempts < 20:\n                    m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_F.shape[0]))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + gen_memory_F[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i > 0.0:\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                F_i_list[i] = F_i\n\n                # Sample CR_i from normal distribution around a random memory entry\n                m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_CR.shape[0]))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.randn() * 0.1 + gen_memory_CR[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                CR_i_list[i] = CR_i\n\n                # Choose p-best from top p*pop_size individuals\n                p_num = max(1, int(round(p_best * pop_size)))\n                p_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_num)])\n                x_best_p = pop[p_idx]\n\n                # Select two distinct random indices from pop and archive\n                # Use union of pop + archive for greater diversity\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if candidates:\n                    r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n\n                # Choose r2 from union(pop, archive), cannot equal i or r1\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    if rng.random() < archive_prob:\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\n                        x_r2 = archive[r2]\n                    else:\n                        r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                        x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n\n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_best_p - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip to bounds\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors with JADE selection and archive updates\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with archive\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old position before replacing\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_size:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n                    else:\n                        # Replace a random archive member\n                        replace_pos = int(rng.randint(0, archive_size))\n                        archive[replace_pos] = pop[i]\n\n                    # Track successful parameter values\n                    SF.append(F_i_list[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_i_list[i])\n                    S_delta.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE success-history memory\n            if len(SF) > 0:\n                deltas = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                weights = deltas / (np.sum(deltas) + 1e-12)\n\n                # Lehmer mean for F\n                SF_arr = np.array(SF, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr * SF_arr))\n                denom_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr))\n                if denom_F > 1e-12:\n                    memory_F[memory_pos] = min(1.0, max(0.0, sum_F / denom_F))\n\n                # Weighted mean for CR (standard mean)\n                SCR_arr = np.array(SCR, dtype=float)\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(np.sum(weights * SCR_arr), 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_len\n\n            # Update stagnation counter based on this generation's improvement\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n                stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                stagnation_count += 1\n\n            # Partial diversity restart when the run has stalled for a while.\n            # Replace the worst half of the population with fresh random points.\n            if stagnation_count >= 15 and evals < self.max_evals - pop_size:\n                restart_count = max(1, pop_size // 2)\n                # Keep the current global best individual in the population\n                cur_best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n                if gbest_f < fitness[cur_best_idx]:\n                    pop[cur_best_idx] = gbest.copy()\n                    fitness[cur_best_idx] = gbest_f\n                # Replace the worst individuals (excluding the preserved best) with random points\n                order = np.argsort(fitness)\n                worst_idx = order[-restart_count:]\n                for idx in worst_idx:\n                    if idx == cur_best_idx:\n                        continue\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                        break\n                    new_x = rng.uniform(lb, ub)\n                    pop[idx] = new_x\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, new_x)\n                    evals += 1\n                stagnation_count = 0\n                # Re-sort for next iteration's p-best selection\n                sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

 if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim,
